**[00:05:42.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000104)  
The breakdown of the budget, and we have made a general breakdown here in this slide,

**[00:05:50.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000105)  
shows that the budget is divided into several components.

**[00:06:00.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000106)  
First of all, the budget covers the organizational outcome one,

**[00:06:07.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000107)  
which is really the repair, maintenance, and the building of national roads and bridges,

**[00:06:13.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000108)  
or the National Roads and Bridges Program.

**[00:06:16.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000109)  
Roughly 200 billion.

**[00:06:20.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000110)  
The Foreign Assisted Projects Program, these are covered by the various official development assistance loans,

**[00:06:31.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000111)  
and comprise of the big ticket flagship projects, not only of the PWH, but of the entire government.

**[00:06:41.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000112)  
Roughly about 118 billion.

**[00:06:49.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000113)  
The, and for this year, as we all know po, nung 2026,

**[00:06:56.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000114)  
dinesisyonan po ng both executive at ng kongreso na wala munang flood mitigation program na locally funded.

**[00:07:06.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000115)  
Kaya, pero po ngayong 2027,

**[00:07:07.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000116)  
2027 meron na po, at ang locally funded projects po natin for 2027 ay 83.84 billion.

**[00:07:18.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000117)  
Pero meron din po tayong Foreign Assisted Ongoing Flood Mitigation Projects.

**[00:07:26.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000118)  
Kasama po ito dun sa 117 billion Foreign Assisted Projects worth 19 billion.

**[00:07:34.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000119)  
Meron din po tayong Basic Infrastructure Program.

**[00:07:42.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000120)  
Divided into 105 billion for access roads and bridges.

**[00:07:49.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000121)  
From the national roads leading to major strategic facilities.

**[00:07:54.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000122)  
Meron po tayong 40.97 billion multipurpose buildings to support social services.

**[00:08:02.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000123)  
Meron din po tayong coastal roads to augment resiliency na 1.38 billion.

**[00:08:10.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000124)  
And meron din po tayong 419 million for local ports.

**[00:08:15.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000125)  
These four components form part of the Basic Infrastructure Program.

**[00:08:20.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000126)  
Meron din po 510 million na construction and rehabilitation for PWDs and elderlies as mandated by law.

**[00:08:33.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000127)  
Meron din po tayong 1 billion rehabilitation of disaster infrastructure.

**[00:08:38.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000128)  
Ito po yung ating commonly termed as yung,

**[00:08:40.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000129)  
Quick Response Fund natin during calamities.

**[00:08:44.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000130)  
And meron din pong 7.29 billion na construction and rehabilitation of water supply as mandated by the 1989 Republic Act on Water Supply.

**[00:08:58.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000131)  
Yung batas po natin on water supply.

**[00:09:02.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000132)  
Apart from this po, meron din po tayo yung ating automatic appropriations which essentially cover,

**[00:09:13.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000133)  
personal services, maintenance and other operating expenses.

**[00:09:18.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000134)  
And meron din po tayong support to operations.

**[00:09:22.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000135)  
Ito po yung mga feasibility studies natin, mga detailed engineering natin,

**[00:09:27.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000136)  
mga ating mga studies to make plans and programs.

**[00:09:32.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000137)  
Right of way worth 27.3 billion.

**[00:09:35.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000138)  
And finally yung general administration and support po na 2.77 billion.

**[00:09:40.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000139)  
The details of which we can provide the committee as well.

**[00:09:43.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000140)  
And which has already provided in the current draft of the, in the National Expenditure Program.

**[00:09:52.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000141)  
So this gives us a bird's eye view po.

**[00:09:54.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000142)  
Now if kumamagapatin niyo po, I will now proceed with the budget reforms that we have initiated over the course of the past 11 months.

**[00:10:06.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000143)  
Unang-una po, mag-start po tayo sa basics.

**[00:10:09.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000144)  
Uunahin ko na po na skin-wrap na po natin yung formula na,

**[00:10:14.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000145)  
yung tinatawag nilang parametric formula na ginagamit nung nakaraang mga ilang taon.

**[00:10:21.470] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000146)  
We simplified the allocations to be based on three core components.

**[00:10:27.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000147)  
First of all po, ang utos po ng ating Pangulo,

**[00:10:31.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000148)  
i-prioritize yung mga nakatenga o hindi tapos.

**[00:10:36.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000149)  
So we are prioritizing, trying to complete in some cases within the next fiscal year,

**[00:10:42.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000150)  
in most cases, dahil po maglalaki pa ang requirements,

**[00:10:45.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000151)  
in succeeding years, yung mga nakatenga o unfinished nating mga proyekto.

**[00:10:51.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000152)  
Ikalawa po, importante din po sa Pangulo, ay yung mandato ng DPWH na i-prioritize ang maintenance and repair of critical infrastructure.

**[00:11:02.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000153)  
Ito yung ating mga national roads and national bridges.

**[00:11:08.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000154)  
At kasama na rin po yung mga flood mitigation infrastructures na nasisira.

**[00:11:14.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000155)  
Nakita naman natin nitong mga nakaraang mga dumaan na bagyo.

**[00:11:17.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000156)  
Kailangan din po i-repair po yan ng mabilisan.

**[00:11:20.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000157)  
And i-address din po yung immediate needs ng mga communities,

**[00:11:28.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000158)  
which are covered mostly by the basic infrastructure program o yung BIT program natin.

**[00:11:37.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000159)  
Base po dito, nag-implement po tayo ng very, very strict evaluation

**[00:11:42.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000160)  
project evaluation system.

**[00:11:47.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000161)  
Unang-una po, pinagakas po natin ang technical and project vetting.

**[00:11:52.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000162)  
Makikita po natin ito sa mga susunod na slide kung ano po ang ginawa natin

**[00:11:57.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000163)  
para masigurado na ang mga project ay talagang na-vet ng maayos.

**[00:12:02.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000164)  
Yung pagsisigurado po, alimbawa, na mayroong mga plano, lahat ng mga project,

**[00:12:06.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000165)  
hindi po tulad ng dati na madalas ang nangyayari, project title lang po at cost ang pumapasok

**[00:12:13.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000166)  
sa NEP.

**[00:12:13.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000167)  
Wala po yung marami po dyan.

**[00:12:16.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000168)  
Wala pong mga plano, wala pong mga FS, hindi po basis sa mga existing master plans natin.

**[00:12:22.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000169)  
Ikarawa po, we are improving project information and traceability.

**[00:12:30.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000170)  
I-upload po namin, hindi lang po yung mga project titles na nandito sa NEP,

**[00:12:36.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000171)  
kundi i-upload din po namin lahat po ng supporting documents ng lahat ng mga project

**[00:12:42.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000172)  
na nakapaloob sa NEP para po makita lang, hindi lamang ng ating honorable committee,

**[00:12:48.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000173)  
pero makita din po ng buong publiko.

**[00:12:51.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000174)  
At ikatro po, very, very strict po tayo ngayon sa mga mandatory documentary requirements

**[00:12:58.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000175)  
which I will expound on in the coming slides.

**[00:13:01.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000176)  
Sinisigurado na po natin ngayon, kagaya nung nasabi ko kanina,

**[00:13:05.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000177)  
na hindi lang project title at cost ang nakapaloob sa National Expenditure Program.

**[00:13:12.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000178)  
Nandito na rin po ang lahat ng supporting mandatory requirements ng bawat proyekto.

**[00:13:20.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000179)  
At ito po, next slide, ito po ang stringent project requirements po natin ngayon.

**[00:13:26.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000180)  
Lahat po ng proyekto na nakapaloob dito sa National Expenditure Program natin

**[00:13:31.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000181)  
ay dapat merong approved program of work.

**[00:13:36.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000182)  
Ito po yung kabilin-bilina ng ating Pangulo, dahil nga po siya mismo nakita niya for himself,

**[00:13:43.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000183)  
na kapag po nung nag-expect siya po siya ng mga iba't ibang proyekto,

**[00:13:49.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000184)  
madalas po pag humihingi siya ng plano, wala pong ma-produce na plano ang DPWH.

**[00:13:53.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000185)  
At ito po ay nasa level na ng implementation.

**[00:13:57.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000186)  
Ito po, rinirequire na po natin ang plano,

**[00:13:59.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000187)  
ngayong parang po na ipapaloob pa natin lang sa budget ang mga proyektong nakapaloob sa National Expenditure Program.

**[00:14:09.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000188)  
Ikaliwa po, yung validated geo-tagged photos.

**[00:14:14.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000189)  
I will, if you will allow me to explain ano ang pagkakaiba po nung ginawa namin ngayon dun sa ginawa dati.

**[00:14:21.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000190)  
Dati po, and I think we have all seen this in the height of the flood control scandal,

**[00:14:27.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000191)  
madalas po sa mga geo-tagging na nangyari o mga coordinates na nangyari,

**[00:14:36.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000192)  
pagdating po sa pre-planning, sa planning stage, meaning sa pagpasok po ng mga proyekto sa budget,

**[00:14:44.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000193)  
either wala pong coordinates or kung meron man, ito po ay tinatawag nating tabletop coordinates lang.

**[00:14:55.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000194)  
Meaning, ginugel map, tapos inupload po.

**[00:15:00.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000195)  
At marami pa po sa mga proyekto, wala. Ni ito, wala. Noong itong nakaraan.

**[00:15:04.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000196)  
Ngayon po, dito sa mga proyektong pinasok natin sa National Expenditure Program, required po ang geotag photos on-site.

**[00:15:15.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000197)  
Meaning po, alam naman po natin, sanay naman po tayo sa mga apps natin sa telepono,

**[00:15:19.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000198)  
required po silang mag-upload on-site ng geotag photos na nandun mismo ang picture ng lugar,

**[00:15:27.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000199)  
kasama yung coordinates na geotag mismo.

**[00:15:32.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000200)  
So, hindi na po pwede yung nasa opisina lang po sila at google map sila at igagagay nila kung saan saan na lang.

**[00:15:40.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000201)  
Hindi na po pwede yun.

**[00:15:41.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000202)  
The project engineers, the district engineers, the planning engineers need to go on-site

**[00:15:48.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000203)  
and photograph the site with the coordinates attached automatically to the photos.

**[00:15:56.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000204)  
Required po yan.

**[00:15:58.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000205)  
Ikatlo po, requirement po ito ng Department of Budget Management,

**[00:16:03.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000206)  
yung required BP-202 compliance forms na nakapaloob po dyan lahat ng information tungkol sa proyekto.

**[00:16:11.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000207)  
Ikaapat po, and I will again discuss this in depth in one slide,

**[00:16:16.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000208)  
required pong pumirma lahat ng heads of implementation offices, district engineers and regional directors

**[00:16:24.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000209)  
ng tinatawag na Certificate of Implementation.

**[00:16:28.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000210)  
Ano po itong Certificate of Implementability?

**[00:16:30.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000211)  
Siguro we can move to the next slide, I think.

**[00:16:35.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000212)  
Yan po, babalik na lang po ako dun sa previous slide.

**[00:16:39.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000213)  
Kailangan po ng certificate na sinasabi na walang right-of-way issue.

**[00:16:45.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000214)  
Kasi madalas po ang napansin namin nung kami po ay pumasok late last year

**[00:16:50.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000215)  
na marami pong mga proyekto ay nare-render unimplementable

**[00:16:55.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000216)  
dahil po, linalagay ang proyekto sa budget

**[00:16:57.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000217)  
na

**[00:16:58.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000218)  
hindi pa po secure ang right-of-way

**[00:17:00.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000219)  
o walang kasigurahan na makukuha ang right-of-way

**[00:17:03.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000220)  
within the implementation period of the project.

**[00:17:07.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000221)  
Kaya po maraming mga nasasayang ng napondo.

**[00:17:10.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000222)  
So kailangan pong i-certify nung implementation office

**[00:17:14.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000223)  
na walang right-of-way issues.

**[00:17:17.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000224)  
Kailangan po din lang i-certify na no duplicate projects

**[00:17:21.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000225)  
dun sa mga project na yan.

**[00:17:23.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000226)  
Ito din po ay madalas na nakikita natin

**[00:17:25.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000227)  
ng mga nakaraang mga taon na madalas

**[00:17:27.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000228)  
same project titles,

**[00:17:29.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000229)  
ilang beses pong lumarabas

**[00:17:31.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000230)  
sa National Expenditure Program.

**[00:17:32.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000231)  
So, kaya i-certify na po ito ng DE

**[00:17:36.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000232)  
o ng RD

**[00:17:37.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000233)  
na wala pong duplicate project po ito.

**[00:17:41.870] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000234)  
Wala din pong previous funded

**[00:17:43.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000235)  
except for yung mga for completion.

**[00:17:47.290] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000236)  
At naging stricto na po kami

**[00:17:50.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000237)  
na kailangan po kung isang project

**[00:17:53.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000238)  
ay either continuation or for completion

**[00:17:57.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000239)  
kailangan po nakasaad yan doon sa project title.

**[00:18:00.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000240)  
Para po nakikita po talaga na ito ay ongoing project

**[00:18:04.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000241)  
at either tinutuloy or ikokompleto na lang.

**[00:18:08.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000242)  
And finally po, kagaya po nung geotag locations

**[00:18:11.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000243)  
kailangan pong i-certify nila

**[00:18:14.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000244)  
yung identified specific locations.

**[00:18:18.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000245)  
At ito po ay pinipirma ng DE

**[00:18:20.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000246)  
para po yung accountability ay klarong-klaro.

**[00:18:23.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000247)  
At kung meron pong madiskubre na problema,

**[00:18:27.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000248)  
makikita po ng COA at ng mga investigative authorities

**[00:18:31.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000249)  
na pinirmaan po mismo yan

**[00:18:33.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000250)  
at accountable po klaro

**[00:18:35.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000251)  
ang implementing office

**[00:18:37.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000252)  
either the DE or the regional director

**[00:18:40.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000253)  
dahil meron po siyang signed certificate of inventibility.

**[00:18:44.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000254)  
And ito po ay may kasama na rin pong siyempre

**[00:18:47.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000255)  
ano ito, accountability at takot.

**[00:18:50.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000256)  
Kasi siyempre po, kapag pumirma na po ang DE dyan o RD

**[00:18:54.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000257)  
eh matatakot na po yung gumawa ng house shout,

**[00:18:57.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000258)  
na proyekto.

**[00:18:58.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000259)  
Dahil po talagang nakapirma na siya dyan

**[00:19:00.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000260)  
at lahat po yan ay nakasubmit

**[00:19:02.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000261)  
bago ipasok sa National Expenditure Program.

**[00:19:06.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000262)  
Finally po, one slide up, sorry.

**[00:19:14.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000263)  
Kailangan po ang project ay may basihan at may koordinasyon.

**[00:19:21.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000264)  
Unang-una po ang nakita naming naging maraking problema

**[00:19:25.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000265)  
dun sa mga proyekto pong nababoy

**[00:19:28.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000266)  
ay unang-una,

**[00:19:29.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000267)  
yan ay hindi po,

**[00:19:30.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000268)  
hindi naging base sa either

**[00:19:33.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000269)  
isang existing river basin master plan,

**[00:19:37.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000270)  
isang feasibility study,

**[00:19:39.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000271)  
o drainage master plan ng isang LGU.

**[00:19:43.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000272)  
Inaamin po namin at I'm sure po marami pong magtatanong

**[00:19:46.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000273)  
sa ating honorable members,

**[00:19:48.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000274)  
ano ang status ng mga iba't ibang master plan?

**[00:19:51.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000275)  
And I will be able to answer that very clearly po mamaya,

**[00:19:54.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000276)  
pero sasabihin ko na po ngayon sa presentation ko po pa lang

**[00:19:57.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000277)  
na hindi pa po kumpleto ang ating master plan.

**[00:20:01.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000278)  
Nationwide.

**[00:20:02.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000279)  
Marami pong mga river basin master plans natin ay either hindi pa tapos

**[00:20:08.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000280)  
or kung tapos man, very outdated na po ito.

**[00:20:11.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000281)  
Alam naman po natin, given what we have seen with climate change,

**[00:20:17.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000282)  
the updating of these master plans is critical because they have a direct impact

**[00:20:22.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000283)  
on the engineering designs of the infrastructure that needs to be built.

**[00:20:27.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000284)  
Halimbawa po, kung ang rainfall, average rainfall natin,

**[00:20:31.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000285)  
according to our scientist ex noong 2010, 2013, 2015,

**[00:20:39.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000286)  
the years of which marami sa mga master plans natin eh ganun si kakatanda,

**[00:20:45.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000287)  
magkakipa na po ang pinagbago niyan 10 years or 15 years after.

**[00:20:51.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000288)  
And meron po yung direct impact, halimbawa po sa taas ng mga dike,

**[00:20:54.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000289)  
sa taas ng mga dam, sa design ng mga dike, sa design ng mga dam.

**[00:21:00.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000290)  
Sa laki po ng mga detention basin.

**[00:21:03.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000291)  
So hindi po tayo pwedeng basta-basta na lang magbase sa mga lumang mga studies

**[00:21:10.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000292)  
dahil maraki na po ang pinagbago ng klima 10, 15 years since these plans were completed.

**[00:21:19.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000293)  
Ikalawa po, hindi po lahat ng lugar ay may master plan. Sad to say.

**[00:21:23.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000294)  
Halimbawa po sa NCR, wala pa po talagang NCR fraud master plan.

**[00:21:30.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000295)  
Lahat po ito ay ongoing pa pero meron naman pong mga local master plans

**[00:21:36.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000296)  
tulad po ng Quezon City, halimbawa.

**[00:21:39.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000297)  
Tulad po ng Maynila, tulad po ng Pasig, Marikina.

**[00:21:44.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000298)  
Meron na pong mga master plans yan.

**[00:21:46.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000299)  
Kaya po puwapasok yung requirement din for either Regional Development Council,

**[00:21:54.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000300)  
Provincial Development Council, Local Development Council,

**[00:21:58.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000301)  
or at the very least

**[00:22:00.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000303)  
certificates of no objection from the LGUs para naman po coordinated naman po ang mga proyekto ng DPWH sa ating mga regional, provincial, at local authorities.

**[00:22:21.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000304)  
Hindi na po masasabi alimbawa ng Quezon City na ni Mayor Joy na daang-daang at bilyong-bilyong mga proyekto na fraud mitigation, eh hindi man lang niya alam.

**[00:22:36.750] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000305)  
So hindi na po ipapwede yun kasi required na po itong mga ito. Kailangan po may basehan na.

**[00:22:44.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000306)  
So yun po, very stringent po yan. At yung mga requirements na yan, ipopost po natin at iaralagay.

**[00:22:51.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000307)  
Iaralagay po natin para makita po ng publiko for full transparency.

**[00:22:57.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000308)  
Next slide.

**[00:23:00.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000309)  
Opo, importante din po itong budget rationalization and prioritization.

**[00:23:04.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000310)  
And one major component of this is the rationalization of the convergence and special support program.

**[00:23:16.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000311)  
Because we also, in the course of our...

**[00:23:20.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000312)  
Pushing for reform, nakita po din namin na itong CSSP na tinatawag, maraming din pong pwedeng gawin para ma-streamline natin, ma-rationalize at ma-reforma.

**[00:23:33.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000313)  
Unang-una po, lahat po ng local projects, kami po ay ginagay na po namin sa isang basic infrastructure program na lang.

**[00:23:45.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000314)  
Wala na po yung sipag.

**[00:23:47.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000315)  
So isa na lang po, VIP na lang po tayo para...

**[00:23:51.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000316)  
It is also meant to avoid duplication.

**[00:23:53.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000317)  
Dahil doon po sa mga nakita na din natin, na pinoint out din po ng mga investigations, ay doon din po nang gagaling yung mga duplication.

**[00:24:02.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000318)  
So simply, the simple solution was just to put everything under the VIP.

**[00:24:09.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000319)  
We have also instituted the removal of all the convergence programs with the other departments.

**[00:24:19.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000320)  
Kaya makikita nyo po dyan, halimbawa.

**[00:24:20.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000321)  
'no?

**[00:24:21.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000322)  
Ang trip program with the tourism department.

**[00:24:25.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000323)  
Tinanggal na po namin yun.

**[00:24:27.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000324)  
At ang sabi po ng DBM, imbes na po sa DPWH ipasok, yung mga programa ng DOT for infrastructure,

**[00:24:39.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000325)  
igagay na po either sa TIEZA or sa DOT na.

**[00:24:43.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000326)  
Bakit po?

**[00:24:44.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000327)  
Dahil po marami din po kaming narinig at nakita na priority ng DOT,

**[00:24:49.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000328)  
pero hindi naman po pumapasok sa trip program ng DPWH.

**[00:24:54.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000329)  
At meron pong mga pumasok sa trip program na hindi naman po priority ng DOT.

**[00:25:01.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000330)  
Kapareho din po ng roll-it program ng DTI.

**[00:25:05.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000331)  
Meron pong mga priorities ang DTI na hindi naman po pumapasok sa DPWH roll-it program.

**[00:25:12.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000332)  
So again, the simple solution was to just lodge all these priority infrastructure programs of DTI,

**[00:25:21.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000333)  
DTI, DOT, DOTR, yung katuparan program, and others, sa kanya-kanya na lang po nilang mga department.

**[00:25:29.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000334)  
At kung gusto po nila na ang DPWH ang mag-implement doon,

**[00:25:34.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000335)  
then ita-download na lang po yung pondong yun at magkakaroon na lang po ng memorandum of agreement between the agencies.

**[00:25:42.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000336)  
Again, to make it more in tune with the priorities of those departments

**[00:25:49.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000337)  
and

**[00:25:49.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000338)  
at the same time, for full transparency.

**[00:25:52.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000339)  
So, yun po ang ginawa natin sa convergence and social special support program.

**[00:26:05.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000340)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:26:08.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000341)  
Okay, and this is I think important to highlight.

**[00:26:13.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000342)  
Typically po, ang average projects ng DPWH

**[00:26:18.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000343)  
ay umaabot ng mahigit.

**[00:26:21.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000344)  
20,000 projects.

**[00:26:22.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000345)  
In the cases po, in 2024 and 2025,

**[00:26:26.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000346)  
nung napakataas po, halos isang triyon ang budget ng DPWH,

**[00:26:31.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000347)  
ay umaabot po yan ng mahigit 25,000 projects.

**[00:26:34.750] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000348)  
Ngayon po, talaga pong

**[00:26:36.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000349)  
nag-sumikap kami

**[00:26:39.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000350)  
na talagang ibaba po yung number of projects natin.

**[00:26:44.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000351)  
And there are several reasons for this.

**[00:26:47.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000352)  
Unang-una po is, number one,

**[00:26:48.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000353)  
number one,

**[00:26:49.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000354)  
we eliminated or tried our best

**[00:26:54.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000355)  
to eliminate all duplicate projects, number one,

**[00:26:59.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000356)  
and all overlapping projects.

**[00:27:02.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000357)  
Number two,

**[00:27:03.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000358)  
we really wanted to focus on the big ticket projects

**[00:27:08.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000359)  
and bring down the number of small ticket projects.

**[00:27:13.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000360)  
In fact po, for the BIP program,

**[00:27:16.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000361)  
of course, this is still,

**[00:27:18.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000362)  
we leave it up to the wisdom of the committee on this.

**[00:27:21.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000363)  
But BPM mandated to us,

**[00:27:23.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000364)  
under the BIP program,

**[00:27:25.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000365)  
to remove all projects below 5 million pesos.

**[00:27:31.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000366)  
And that also drastically reduced the number of projects.

**[00:27:34.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000367)  
And I think the third is also very important.

**[00:27:38.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000368)  
Alam niyo po, Mr. Chairman, Honorable Members,

**[00:27:42.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000369)  
nung pong pumasok ako sa DPWH,

**[00:27:44.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000370)  
isa po sa mga unang tinanong ko sa ating mga implementing offices

**[00:27:48.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000371)  
is,

**[00:27:50.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000372)  
ano ba,

**[00:27:51.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000373)  
ang ideal number of projects na dapat ini-implement

**[00:27:55.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000374)  
ng ating mga implementation offices,

**[00:28:00.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000375)  
yung ating pong mga district engineers and regional directors.

**[00:28:05.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000376)  
Kasi syempre po, kung ako'y district engineer,

**[00:28:08.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000377)  
halimbawa, sa isang distrito,

**[00:28:10.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000378)  
say, sa Iloilo po,

**[00:28:12.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000379)  
kay Deputy Speaker Janet,

**[00:28:16.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000380)  
no,

**[00:28:17.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000381)  
bilang isang district office or district engineer,

**[00:28:20.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000382)  
meron din po,

**[00:28:21.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000383)  
kung absorptive capacity ang distritong iyon.

**[00:28:24.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000384)  
Meaning, there is a limit to the number of projects

**[00:28:28.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000385)  
that can be implemented by a district engineer or a regional office.

**[00:28:36.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000386)  
Nalaman ko po, na lampas-lampas na po pala sa ideal number.

**[00:28:42.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000387)  
I think up to, you say, up to what?

**[00:28:45.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000388)  
Triple, quadruple?

**[00:28:47.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000389)  
In some case, triple, quadruple the ideal.

**[00:28:51.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000390)  
Kunyari po, ang ideal po na ini-implement lang

**[00:28:55.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000391)  
ng isang district engineering office ay,

**[00:28:57.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000392)  
sabihin natin,

**[00:28:58.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000393)  
isang daang proyekto lang,

**[00:29:02.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000394)  
umaabot na po ng times three, times four.

**[00:29:06.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000395)  
So, sinikap din po namin na iba ba ito,

**[00:29:10.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000396)  
kaya po nakikita nyo yung dramatic change

**[00:29:13.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000397)  
sa number of projects, no?

**[00:29:15.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000398)  
Sa number of projects ng DPWH,

**[00:29:20.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000399)  
because of those three reasons.

**[00:29:23.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000400)  
And I think this is very important po, moving forward.

**[00:29:28.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000401)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:29:29.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000402)  
Now, let me go po to the allocations per region.

**[00:29:33.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000403)  
In the last 10 years po, the average allocation for Luzon, Visayas, Mindanao,

**[00:29:41.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000404)  
and we can provide for the regional breakdowns of this,

**[00:29:43.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000405)  
is roughly 56% in Luzon,

**[00:29:47.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000406)  
27% in Mindanao,

**[00:29:50.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000407)  
and only 17% in the Visayas.

**[00:29:53.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000408)  
No, what we tried to do po,

**[00:29:56.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000409)  
dahil nga po we wanted to make this more needs-

**[00:30:07.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000410)  
Ito na po yung ating breakdown ngayon for 2027.

**[00:30:10.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000411)  
We brought it down po sa Luzon from 56% average over the last 10 years.

**[00:30:16.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000412)  
It is now down to 52% roughly.

**[00:30:22.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000413)  
Visayas is now at 20% from only 70% on average.

**[00:30:27.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000414)  
In the last 10 years.

**[00:30:30.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000415)  
And Mindanao po is a little lower po na 27 point.

**[00:30:37.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000416)  
Sorry, it's the same at 28%.

**[00:30:39.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000417)  
So we tried our best po to really make the budget for 2027 more equitable and more needs-based.

**[00:30:49.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000418)  
In fact po, and I'm looking, we tried to look at also the regions with the,

**[00:30:59.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000419)  
highest poverty incidences, namely Region 5, Region 8, Region 13, and Region 9.

**[00:31:12.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000420)  
And tinaas po namin yung budgets din po relative to previous budgets.

**[00:31:18.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000421)  
We can provide po the data.

**[00:31:25.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000422)  
So we know that this is not, still not perfect or it is not,

**[00:31:31.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000423)  
equitable enough, but we tried our best po to start o rin.

**[00:31:35.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000424)  
Trying to make the budget more equitable.

**[00:31:37.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000425)  
But this is a process that will require several years po of correcting,

**[00:31:42.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000426)  
which we believe was, you know, quite a, pasensya na po, no?

**[00:31:46.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000427)  
A more Luzon-centric budget, no?

**[00:31:51.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000428)  
And really try to make the budget more equitable for the regions that really need it the most.

**[00:31:58.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000429)  
Especially in the poorest regions of 5.

**[00:32:01.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000430)  
8, 13, et cetera, and 9, no?

**[00:32:06.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000431)  
Para po naman yung mga mas nangangailangan, e, mas mataas ang kanilang pondo.

**[00:32:10.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000432)  
This we feel is a good start, but personally po, this is still not enough.

**[00:32:15.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000433)  
And we will leave it again up to the wisdom of Congress.

**[00:32:18.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000434)  
If you feel that we need to reallocate some more in order to really address the needs,

**[00:32:25.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000435)  
especially of regions and provinces and areas that need it the most.

**[00:32:33.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000436)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:32:34.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000437)  
And let me now go po to flood mitigation, no?

**[00:32:38.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000438)  
And what the reforms we have tried to initiate, started to initiate,

**[00:32:45.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000439)  
and hopefully we will be able to continue to initiate with the help of Congress in the coming months.

**[00:32:51.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000440)  
As we implement the budget that will be eventually passed by Congress.

**[00:32:58.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000441)  
And these are po the six pillars of our...

**[00:33:02.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000442)  
reforms with respect to flood mitigation.

**[00:33:06.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000443)  
I just want to emphasize, no, that we will try not to use the word flood control anymore.

**[00:33:13.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000444)  
No, we are, we have had a lot of conversations po with the experts, specifically Project NOAA.

**[00:33:19.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000445)  
And we agree with them that we need to use better terminology because words matter and terms matter.

**[00:33:27.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000446)  
And it's really about flood mitigation or flood management.

**[00:33:30.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000447)  
Kasi po, yung control po...

**[00:33:32.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000448)  
It sends, I think, a wrong message kasi hindi naman po natin talaga makokontrol ang pagbahay.

**[00:33:38.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000449)  
Nakita naman po natin kahit sa mga mayayamang bansa, binabaha po sila despite the top class, world class infrastructure in Japan, in other countries.

**[00:33:48.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000450)  
Meron pa rin pong bahay.

**[00:33:49.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000451)  
No, I think we really need to focus on what we can do to mitigate para maibsan po natin yung nararamdaman natin.

**[00:33:57.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000452)  
Obviously po, ang dami po nating problema all over the country which we have seen just in the past couple of...

**[00:34:03.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000453)  
of weeks and months.

**[00:34:05.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000454)  
But I think, no, it's very important na gamitin po natin yung tamang terminology.

**[00:34:10.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000455)  
And according to the experts, especially Project NOAA, flood mitigation is really the proper term.

**[00:34:16.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000456)  
So, ito po yung ating mga reforms, no, na sinisimulan na natin.

**[00:34:24.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000457)  
Hindi pa ito perfect.

**[00:34:25.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000458)  
That's why we will need the help of Congress for this.

**[00:34:28.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000459)  
And we will need the help of our provinces, our LGUs.

**[00:34:33.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000460)  
To help us implement this.

**[00:34:35.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000461)  
But we are trying our best to start with these six pillars.

**[00:34:39.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000462)  
Number one po ay, kailangan po based na sa mga plano.

**[00:34:46.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000463)  
Inaamin po natin na hindi pa kumpleto ang plano.

**[00:34:49.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000464)  
Hindi pa updated ang mga plano.

**[00:34:51.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000465)  
Maraming mga area na wala pang plan at all.

**[00:34:55.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000466)  
Wala pa pong plano.

**[00:34:56.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000467)  
Halimbawa po, sa ibang major river basins natin, wala pa pong master plan.

**[00:35:02.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000468)  
No, there are really just a handful of major river basins na meron ng master plan.

**[00:35:09.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000469)  
No, marami pa pong wala.

**[00:35:10.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000470)  
Marami po po mga LGUs na wala pang drainage master plan.

**[00:35:14.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000471)  
Kaya, importante din po na hingin po namin din ang tulong ng ating committee at ng kongreso.

**[00:35:20.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000472)  
Sana po, bigyan tayo ng mas maraking pondo for project studies, feasibility studies, and master planning.

**[00:35:28.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000473)  
Kasi po, in the past budgets, napansin ko po,

**[00:35:31.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000474)  
mababa po.

**[00:35:33.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000475)  
Ang naibibigay sa DPWH for feasibility studies, detailed engineering.

**[00:35:39.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000476)  
At dahil po doon, nagsasuffer din po talaga.

**[00:35:42.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000477)  
Hindi lang ng pagpaplano ng mga proyekto, kundi yung pag-implement ng mga proyekto.

**[00:35:48.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000478)  
Ikalawa po, we need po to focus on closing the gaps and finishing unfinished flood mitigation projects.

**[00:35:59.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000479)  
Nakikita na po natin ngayon yan all over the country.

**[00:36:02.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000480)  
At nakakalungkot po talaga.

**[00:36:04.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000481)  
Marami sa mga nasangkot na damay sa flood control scandal ay unfinished.

**[00:36:16.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000482)  
Halos lahat po ay unfinished.

**[00:36:18.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000483)  
Ang problema po dyan, kapag ang isang halimbawa, isang dike ay unfinished, meron po yung butas.

**[00:36:25.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000484)  
Tumatango po ang ating kaibigan at si Honorable Congressman Toby Chanko sa navotas.

**[00:36:31.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000485)  
Example po ang navotas dyan.

**[00:36:33.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000486)  
Ang dami pong mga butas,

**[00:36:34.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000487)  
mga gaps.

**[00:36:36.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000488)  
Kaya po nung ako po ay pumasok, sa unang-una pong tumawag sa akin ay si Kong Toby.

**[00:36:42.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000489)  
At ang unang-una po niyang hiningi ay isarado ang mga gap.

**[00:36:47.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000490)  
At ginawan po natin ang paraan.

**[00:36:49.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000491)  
Nag-anap po tayo from savings, nag-reprioritize po tayo para isara ang lahat ng gaps ng navotas.

**[00:36:55.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000492)  
At ang naging immediate effect po noon, especially po, kasama na rin po yung pagpapagawa ng pumping station at mga flood gates,

**[00:37:02.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000493)  
halos wala pong bahasan.

**[00:37:04.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000494)  
Ang dami pong mga butas this year and I think Congressman Toby can attest to that.

**[00:37:08.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000495)  
Pero all over the country po, ang daming butas.

**[00:37:11.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000496)  
Ang daming gaps.

**[00:37:12.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000497)  
Ang problema po dyan, kapag ang isa pong revetment, alimbawa, ay may gap.

**[00:37:16.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000498)  
At alam po nung ating mga, tumatango po ang ating mga members dito.

**[00:37:20.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000499)  
Pag tinamaan po ng bahayan, sigurado po, 100%.

**[00:37:24.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000500)  
Kahit hindi pa substandard yan.

**[00:37:26.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000501)  
Kahit maayos pang pagkagawa niyan.

**[00:37:28.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000502)  
Kung yan ay may butas, yan po ay masisira.

**[00:37:31.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000503)  
Dahil po, pag ang gap ay pinasok ng tubig,

**[00:37:35.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000504)  
papasok po ang tubig doon sa gap na yan at wawasakin po niya yung deking.

**[00:37:39.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000505)  
At yun po ang nakikita na natin na nangyayari all over the country.

**[00:37:43.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000506)  
Kaya very critical po, ang pagsasara ng mga gaps na yan.

**[00:37:47.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000507)  
At medyo, hirap din po kami dahil tali po ang kamay namin sa DPWH.

**[00:37:52.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000508)  
Bakit?

**[00:37:53.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000509)  
Dahil po marami sa mga may gap din ay subject ng investigations po ng iba't ibang ahensya.

**[00:38:01.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000510)  
Tulad ng ombudsman ng DOJ, ng NBI.

**[00:38:04.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000511)  
At ng COA.

**[00:38:05.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000512)  
At kapag po subject ng investigation,

**[00:38:08.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000513)  
hindi po natin basta-basta na lang mapapagawa yung mga proyektong yan.

**[00:38:13.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000514)  
Halimbawa po, sa Bulacan po,

**[00:38:16.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000515)  
na kailangan-kailangan talaga dahil diyan po yung karamihang mga binaboy na proyekto.

**[00:38:21.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000516)  
Hirap na hirap po kami magpaayos ng mga proyektong nababoy.

**[00:38:26.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000517)  
Dahil po, isang katutak po na proseso at requirements po ng COA

**[00:38:31.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000518)  
para po makakuha ng COA clearance para ma-

**[00:38:34.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000519)  
simulan ng pag-repairs.

**[00:38:36.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000520)  
Kaya po, humihingi din po kami ng tulong sa committee, sa kongreso.

**[00:38:40.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000521)  
Baka po matulungan kami dyan.

**[00:38:42.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000522)  
Dahil po, kahit gusto naming ayusin ang mga gap na ito,

**[00:38:45.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000523)  
kung ito po ay subject ng investigation lalo na,

**[00:38:48.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000524)  
at subject ng COA, either suspension or disallowance na,

**[00:38:54.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000525)  
hindi po namin magawa yan ang basta-basta.

**[00:38:57.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000526)  
Ikatlo po, kailangan po, gaya ng sinabi ko kanina,

**[00:39:00.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000527)  
lahat ay may kailangang approved plan na.

**[00:39:04.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000528)  
Hindi na po pe pwede na project title lang at cost.

**[00:39:10.250] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000529)  
Ganyan po ang nangyari dati.

**[00:39:12.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000530)  
Unfortunately.

**[00:39:13.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000531)  
Ngayon po, hindi na po pe pwede yan.

**[00:39:17.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000532)  
Ikatlo po, gusto din po namin mag-focus on integrated water resource management.

**[00:39:21.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000533)  
Kaya importante din po sa amin,

**[00:39:23.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000534)  
ang pagpapagawa ng mga detention at retarding basins at mga dams,

**[00:39:28.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000535)  
na hindi lang po objective ay mag-mitigate ng pagbaha,

**[00:39:33.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000536)  
kundi mag-imbak din ng tubig.

**[00:39:35.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000537)  
Mag-imbak ng tubig para sa irrigation,

**[00:39:38.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000538)  
mag-imbak din po ng tubig para sa water supply po,

**[00:39:42.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000539)  
na in some areas po ay kailangan-kailangan ng mga communities.

**[00:39:47.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000540)  
Ikalima po ay kailangan po coordinated na po talaga sa mga regional development councils natin,

**[00:39:54.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000541)  
sa ating mga provincial governments at local governments.

**[00:39:57.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000542)  
And finally, kailangan po may actual deutaging,

**[00:40:00.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000543)  
hindi lang po yung ginagawa sa opisina,

**[00:40:03.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000544)  
pero kailangan po talaga may retrato at nandun mismo sa area kung saan pinagkuhan.

**[00:40:16.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000545)  
Next slide po.

**[00:40:20.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000546)  
So halimbawa po, I will just give a few examples po,

**[00:40:24.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000547)  
ng mga flood control or flood  sorry, flood mitigation master plans.

**[00:40:29.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000548)

**[00:40:32.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000549)  
Flood mitigation master plans.

**[00:40:35.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000550)  
Halimbawa po,

**[00:40:36.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000551)  
sa NCR.

**[00:40:37.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000552)  
Sa Quezon City po, meron na pong approved Quezon City Drainage Master Plan.

**[00:40:43.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000553)

**[00:40:47.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000554)  
ay nakaakmana po sa Drainage Master Plan ng Quezon City.

**[00:40:53.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000555)

**[00:40:55.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000556)  
which has proven to be quite effective dun sa area ng Tatalon.

**[00:41:00.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000557)  
There has been significant relief in terms of heights of floods

**[00:41:04.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000558)  
and the soil.

**[00:41:06.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000559)  
Speed at which yung flood po e bumaba.

**[00:41:08.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000560)  
Na nakita na po natin.

**[00:41:10.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000561)

**[00:41:16.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000562)

**[00:41:24.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000563)  
dahil po naarang na po ito at naplano na po.

**[00:41:27.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000564)

**[00:41:33.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000565)  
Unfortunately po, nung nakaraan,

**[00:41:36.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000566)

**[00:41:40.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000567)  
eh hindi po nakaakma dito yung mga nagawa nung makaraan.

**[00:41:43.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000568)

**[00:41:52.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000569)

**[00:42:01.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000570)

**[00:42:05.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000571)

**[00:54:50.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000687)
