**[00:54:53.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000688)  
So, paano po poondohan yun?

**[00:54:55.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000689)  
Hindi po pwedeng hindi po poondohan yun agad.

**[00:54:57.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000690)  
So, ang ginawa na lang po namin dito, we used savings.

**[00:55:01.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000691)  
But we feel very strongly that we need a fund that will allow us to address this

**[00:55:07.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000692)  
during situations na wala pong kalamidad.

**[00:55:12.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000693)  
Pag may kalamidad po, okay lang, may QRF tayo.

**[00:55:15.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000694)  
Pero madami po talaga na kahit na walang calamity, bumabagsak na.

**[00:55:19.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000695)  
Tumatangu po si...

**[00:55:22.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000696)  
Deputy Speaker Bambi, kasi I'm sure po sa mga mountainous areas

**[00:55:26.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000697)  
ng Misamis Oriental, nangyayari din po ito.

**[00:55:31.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000698)  
And this is very critical because if these roads are cut off,

**[00:55:36.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000699)  
these communities cannot afford to wait another year

**[00:55:40.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000700)  
na hindi mapopoondohan at maayos itong mga proyektong doon.

**[00:55:44.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000701)  
At kaya naman pong ayusin.

**[00:55:45.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000702)  
Nagimbawa po, sa itong Buda Road, natsira itong October.

**[00:55:49.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000703)  
One month pa rin po ako sa DPWH, nawasak ito.

**[00:55:52.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000704)  
Nasira po ito.

**[00:55:53.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000705)  
Nakahanap po kami ng paraan na fund namin with savings.

**[00:55:57.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000706)  
Nagawa po ito in wala pong dalawang buwan.

**[00:56:01.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000707)  
Naayos po.

**[00:56:01.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000708)  
At ito po yung naayos na nakikita nyo ngayon sa kabigang side.

**[00:56:07.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000709)  
Naopen po namin ang Buda Road in less than two months.

**[00:56:10.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000710)  
But I think we need this.

**[00:56:12.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000711)  
This is a dramatic and very important need that needs to be addressed

**[00:56:17.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000712)  
because this keeps on happening everywhere.

**[00:56:21.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000713)  
Ang isa po,

**[00:56:22.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000714)  
yung madagas ding nangyayari ay yung upgrading ng mga drainage

**[00:56:33.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000715)  
at yung mga tinatawag nilang off-carriageway road safety.

**[00:56:41.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000716)  
Again, pasensya na po, dinatnang-nagang natin na problema to.

**[00:56:44.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000717)  
But I think the districts will know this very well.

**[00:56:47.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000718)  
Marami po sa mga roads, unfortunately, na ginagawa ng DPWH in the past,

**[00:56:51.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000719)  
ginagawa yung kalye, walang drainage.

**[00:56:56.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000720)  
Marami po yan.

**[00:56:57.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000721)  
Even along Mahari Highway, nakakalimutan po ang drainage for whatever reason.

**[00:57:03.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000722)  
So kailangan pong, and again, this is also a reason bakit nasisira ang mga karye ng mabilis.

**[00:57:09.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000723)  
Kasi kapag ang karye po ay walang drainage,

**[00:57:12.910] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000724)  
ang tubig po ay pumupunta sa karye at hindi dahil walang drainage.

**[00:57:17.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000725)  
At dahil po doon, nasisira ang karye ng mabilis.

**[00:57:21.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000726)  
So I think

**[00:57:21.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000727)  
we need to fix this.

**[00:57:24.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000728)  
And also, yung pong safety, yung mga barriers natin.

**[00:57:28.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000729)  
Marami din pong mga areas, again, gago na sa mga mountainous roads,

**[00:57:33.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000730)  
is hindi po nasasama yung mga safety barriers.

**[00:57:37.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000731)  
And this needs, I think, to be addressed.

**[00:57:40.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000732)  
Now, in order po to, anyway, let me proceed po with the next one.

**[00:57:45.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000733)  
Ito, very important din po ito.

**[00:57:48.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000734)  
Yung tulay.

**[00:57:50.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000735)  
And

**[00:57:51.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000736)  
I can see everybody nodding po.

**[00:57:54.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000737)  
Because all over the country, again, unfortunately, dinatang,

**[00:57:59.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000738)  
naran po natin ito, marami po sa mga tugay natin,

**[00:58:02.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000739)  
ay 40 to 50 years old na.

**[00:58:05.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000740)  
All over the country.

**[00:58:07.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000741)  
From Luzon down to Mindanao.

**[00:58:13.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000742)  
At dahil po dyan, marami na pong mga bumabagsak

**[00:58:18.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000743)  
at hindi na po magamit.

**[00:58:19.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000744)  
And these are critical bridges.

**[00:58:22.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000745)  
Along national highways, especially the Maharika N1 network.

**[00:58:28.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000746)  
Ang dami po nyan.

**[00:58:30.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000747)  
Ang problema din po, kapag wala pong pondo para dyan,

**[00:58:36.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000748)  
kailangan pong mag-

**[00:58:38.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000749)  
Halimbawa po, ang isa pong bumagsak na bridge sa Kagayan,

**[00:58:46.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000750)  
sa Northern Luzon, yung Pigatan Bridge.

**[00:58:50.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000751)  
Bumagsak po yan.

**[00:58:50.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000752)  
Dahil sa mga truck.

**[00:58:52.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000753)  
Na-overloaded.

**[00:58:53.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000754)  
Pero dahil, I think it's a 46-year-old bridge.

**[00:58:59.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000755)  
Kung hindi po tayo nakagamit ng savings,

**[00:59:02.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000756)  
hindi po natin na paayos yan temporarily.

**[00:59:05.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000757)  
Pero we cannot, we don't have enough money to keep on doing that

**[00:59:08.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000758)  
every time a bridge collapses or a bridge needs retrofitting.

**[00:59:12.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000759)  
Yung pong Tagloan Bridge.

**[00:59:16.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000760)  
Eight years na po yung kailangan i-retrofit.

**[00:59:20.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000761)  
Pero punyantahan ko po yan with DS Bambi last Thursday.

**[00:59:24.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000762)  
Ngayon po lang po magagawa.

**[00:59:25.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000763)  
Pero kailangan namin gagyan ng augmentation

**[00:59:28.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000764)  
para masigurado na by end of this year,

**[00:59:32.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000765)  
matatapos na yung bridge.

**[00:59:34.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000766)  
Kung hindi po natin nagagyan ng augmentation yan for savings,

**[00:59:36.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000767)  
maghihintay na naman po ng siguro dagawang taon pa.

**[00:59:41.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000768)  
Magiging yung eight years, magiging ten years po.

**[00:59:44.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000769)  
Pero mamamatay na po sa traffic ang mga bumabiyahe sa distrito ni DS Bambi.

**[00:59:50.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000770)  
I saw it for myself with my own eyes.

**[00:59:52.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000771)  
Halos po.

**[00:59:53.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000772)  
Tatlong kilometro po yung traffic ng papuntang butuan and back.

**[00:59:59.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000773)  
Sa summer po, kung nandito po si Kakong Mike, yung Calviga Bridge, yung Giloan Bridge, sarado po yan.

**[01:00:09.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000774)  
Pero ginamitan po natin ang augmentation at ng savings para po mapagawa na.

**[01:00:15.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000775)  
At ngayon po, nabuksa na po natin, na-retrofit na po natin ang Calviga.

**[01:00:19.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000776)  
I think it took five years, I think. Five years na pong nakasarayan.

**[01:00:27.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000777)  
So, kailangan-kailangan po talaga ito.

**[01:00:29.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000778)  
And knock on wood po, pero ano po talaga, marami pong masisira.

**[01:00:37.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000779)  
Just simply because of age of these bridges.

**[01:00:42.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000780)  
Marami po sa mga bridges na ito, nung time pa po ni Pangulong Ferdinand Marcos Sr. pa nagawa.

**[01:00:48.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000781)  
So, you're talking about bridges po that were built in the 60s and the 70s.

**[01:00:52.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000782)  
And they have not been retrofitted since then.

**[01:00:54.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000783)  
They have not been replaced since then.

**[01:00:57.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000784)  
And unfortunately, they have already, they are already have or about to outlive their life.

**[01:01:04.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000785)  
Lalo na po, marami din po tayong problema sa mga overloaded na trucks na hindi din po nakokontrol.

**[01:01:13.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000786)  
Halimbawa po sa pag-itatikatan, dalawa pong 80-ton trucks ang sabay na dumaan dyan.

**[01:01:19.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000787)  
One from each side. Kaya po talaga bumagsak yan.

**[01:01:22.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000788)  
And that is a 46-year-old bridge.

**[01:01:25.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000789)  
So, ano po, kailangan po talaga ito.

**[01:01:31.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000790)  
And finally, ito po, obviously, the flood mitigation master plan.

**[01:01:38.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000791)  
And these are just examples po of the flood mitigation or flood management.

**[01:01:43.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000792)  
And these are things po that we tried to immediately repair and rehabilitate.

**[01:01:49.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000793)  
Nasabi ko na po yung sanavotas na inayos po natin, again, through augmentation and savings.

**[01:01:56.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000794)  
We are right now rebuilding.

**[01:01:58.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000795)  
We are rebuilding the seawall in Albay.

**[01:02:02.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000796)  
Next po, we are doing various open kontrabaha.

**[01:02:06.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000797)  
By the way, these are all unfunded po.

**[01:02:09.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000798)  
We are just trying to use whatever funds that we have from savings for open kontrabaha.

**[01:02:18.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000799)  
By the way po, if I may emphasize on this.

**[01:02:22.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000800)  
Dredging po and desilting of rivers is critical to flood mitigation and management.

**[01:02:29.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000801)  
Unfortunately po, for the last, I think, more than 12 years, and Chairman Romy po can attest to this,

**[01:02:40.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000802)  
halos wala pong dredging at desilting na ginawa ang DPWH.

**[01:02:45.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000803)  
Lalo na sa mga major rivers.

**[01:02:47.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000804)  
That is 12 years na napuno na po ang ating mga waterways at rivers.

**[01:02:54.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000805)  
At dahil po dyan,

**[01:02:56.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000806)  
Baha po talaga,

**[01:02:57.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000807)  
Lahat.

**[01:02:59.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000808)  
Ang imbawa po, I'll just give a few examples.

**[01:03:02.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000809)  
Sa Bicol, sa Naga, which is a Camarines Sur, which is a major basin.

**[01:03:08.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000810)  
Dyan po lahat ng bagsak ng tubig ng Bicol.

**[01:03:10.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000811)  
Sa catch basin po ang Camarines Sur at Naga.

**[01:03:15.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000812)  
Sinabi po sa akin ni Gov, nila Mayor, nung ating mga congressman dyan,

**[01:03:20.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000813)  
wala pong dredging dyan na nangyari in the last 10 years.

**[01:03:24.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000814)  
Wala po talaga.

**[01:03:25.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000815)  
Ngayon lang po tayo nagsisimula.

**[01:03:28.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000816)  
Pero ang problema po, wala ding pondo. So we are made to also resort to augmentation and savings for this.

**[01:03:34.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000817)  
Sa Jensan po at sa Davao, nung ako po'y pumunta sa Talomo at sa Makar sa Jensan, yung river po sa Makar sa Jensan, kung ikaw po ay tatayo sa riverbed, maaabot nyo na po yung tulay.

**[01:03:51.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000818)  
At ako, hindi naman po ako congressman Richard Gomez na matangkat. Mag-iitgang po ako. Pero naaabot ko po mismo yung tulay from the riverbed.

**[01:04:04.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000819)  
Gano'n na po kasilted yung Makar sa Jensan. Wala pong dredging na nangyari doon. At I'm sure po lahat tayo dito, all over, kailangan po natin mag-dredge.

**[01:04:15.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000820)  
Kasi kung wala pong dredging, 10-12 years na po yan, nasisilt ang ating mga waterways.

**[01:04:21.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000821)  
Tagal ka pong babae ng mga areas. Kasi wala na pong pupuntahan ng tubig.

**[01:04:25.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000822)  
Ngayon naman po, yung ating mga success stories, just in a couple of months since we launched it in late November, itong open contra baja natin,

**[01:04:35.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000823)  
we have anecdotal stories from Cagayan, for example po, sa Tugay Garaw.

**[01:04:43.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000824)  
Na-minimize po ang baha sa Tugay Garaw. Dahil po talagang grabe po ang dredging natin since December dyan.

**[01:04:49.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000825)  
Sa mouth ng Tugay Garaw Riverbed.

**[01:04:51.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000826)  
However, dahil po dyan, lumalabas na po ang tubig.

**[01:04:56.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000827)  
Ganyan din po ang nakikita natin sa ibang lugar.

**[01:04:58.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000828)  
So, we really just have to...

**[01:05:00.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000829)  
Sa Maguindanao din po, sabi po ni Congressman Toto, Mangudadato,

**[01:05:04.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000830)  
ang laki po nang nakita nilang improvement sa levels ng floodwaters.

**[01:05:10.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000831)  
And typically po, ang Central Mindanao, grabe po ang baha dyan.

**[01:05:13.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000832)  
Dahil dyan po lahat ng bagsak ng tubig from Central Mindanao, from Bukidnon, Davao, Cotabato, dyan po lahat.

**[01:05:21.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000833)  
Sa Mindanao at sa Cotabato, ang bagsak ng tubig.

**[01:05:24.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000834)  
So, we really need to do more of this.

**[01:05:28.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000835)  
And, of course, we have other special support programs.

**[01:05:32.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000836)  
In particular po, and I just want to emphasize this,

**[01:05:35.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000837)  
marami po tayong mga bridge, sa limbawa, ditong sa Bungabong sa Oriental Mindoro.

**[01:05:42.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000838)  
And we have seen this all on social media.

**[01:05:44.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000839)  
These are bridges that are just being built by the communities.

**[01:05:49.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000840)  
Mostly being used by students.

**[01:05:52.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000841)  
We have to fix this po.

**[01:05:54.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000842)  
Meron po kaming ginawa nito sa Adno sa Pangasinan.

**[01:05:58.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000843)  
I don't know if we have pictures of that.

**[01:06:00.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000844)  
But, nakakaawa po talaga.

**[01:06:03.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000845)  
And we need a program for this.

**[01:06:04.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000846)  
To build these makeshift bridges.

**[01:06:06.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000847)  
And to already build very long, yung mga tatagal na pong mga tulay na matibay na.

**[01:06:11.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000848)  
Para po sa mga studyante, para sa mga communities po,

**[01:06:15.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000849)  
na makakadaan po ang tao, makakadaan po ang trice.

**[01:06:18.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000850)  
Para po si Kel, para lang po madaanan ang mga iba't-ibang mga kababayan na.

**[01:06:26.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000851)  
And finally po, yung National Building Program.

**[01:06:29.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000852)  
This is the last slide po, Mr. Pasensya na po, medyo tumagal.

**[01:06:33.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000853)  
Nakailangan din po natin, marami pong mga unfinished na buildings tayo.

**[01:06:39.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000854)  
From national to local.

**[01:06:41.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000855)  
At yun nga po, nakakagungkot lang po dahil sa sobrang dami,

**[01:06:46.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000856)  
kailangan din po talagang i-prioritize kung ano ang uunahin.

**[01:06:50.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000857)  
Kaya kailangan po talaga ang priority natin, yung mga ongoing para po matapos na.

**[01:06:56.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000858)  
Marami po dyan, from national government agencies to schools, to hospitals,

**[01:07:02.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000859)  
to multi-purpose buildings all over the country, kailangan po natin tapusin yan.

**[01:07:09.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000860)  
So, and this is where we will need your help po para ma-prioritize at masigurado pong matapos ang mga building na ito.

**[01:07:18.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000861)  
Again po,

**[01:07:19.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000862)  
We submit to the wisdom of Congress.

**[01:07:22.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000863)  
This is our proposal.

**[01:07:23.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000864)  
It is by no means perfect.

**[01:07:26.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000865)  
Hindi po namin sinasabi yun.

**[01:07:28.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000866)  
But we feel that it is a start really to try and institute these long and much needed reforms in the DPWH.

**[01:07:37.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000867)  
Not only to ensure na maproteksyonan ng pera ng mga kababayan natin from what we have seen already in the past.

**[01:07:45.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000868)  
And I'm not just talking about the recent flood control scandal.

**[01:07:49.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000869)  
But also to really build the right infrastructure na talagang kailangan po ng mga kababayan natin.

**[01:07:56.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000870)  
With that po, Mr. Chairman, honorable members, I will end my presentation.

**[01:08:02.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000871)  
But any additional information po, we will provide immediately po to the honorable members of the committee.

**[01:08:09.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000872)  
Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[01:08:12.360] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000873)  
Thank you. Thank you very much, Secretary Vince, for that very short presentation.

**[01:08:17.660] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000874)  
And before we proceed with the question and answer,

**[01:08:20.780] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000875)  
I would like to remind our distinguished colleague that each member is given five minutes for questioning and interpellation,

**[01:08:28.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000876)  
excluding the response of the resource person.

**[01:08:30.800] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000877)

**[01:08:38.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000878)

**[01:08:48.760] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000879)  
and followed by the independent and majority members in alternating manner.

**[01:08:55.040] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000880)  
Before we'll call on the first member of the House to ask questions from the minority,

**[01:09:02.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000881)  
I would like to request our Comsec to acknowledge those who came in late.

Rep. Antonino Roman

**[01:10:01.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000905)  
And to start with the question and answer from the minority,

**[01:10:05.420] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000906)  
I would like to call on our minority leader, Marcelino Libanan.

**[01:10:13.240] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000907)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:10:16.480] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000908)  
Mr. Chairman, salamat sa magandang presentation ng

**[01:10:22.640] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000909)  
Secretary of Public Works and Highways.

**[01:10:28.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000910)  
Mr. Chairman, dito sa proposed National Road System,

**[01:10:33.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000911)  
mayroong 199.58 billion.

**[01:10:35.159] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000912)  
Kasama ba dito ang pag-four lanes ng Maharlika Highway?

**[01:10:43.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000913)  
Kasi napakaganda na yung ginawa ninyo sa Maharlika Highway.

**[01:10:47.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000914)  
Sana tuloy-tuloy na po. Pero may mga two lanes pa rin.

**[01:10:49.380] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000915)  
Sana matuloy po natin maging four lanes.

**[01:10:52.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000916)  
Lalo na yung Samar area, maganda na.

**[01:10:55.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000917)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chairman, Minority Leader.

**[01:11:00.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000918)  
Meron pong mga segments na may widening tayo.

**[01:11:04.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000919)  
Pero I have to admit po, ako po mismo ang nagsabi na

**[01:11:10.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000920)  
i-minimize natin yung widening. And I will respond po.

**[01:11:15.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000921)  
Kasi po, sabi ko po sa mga regions po,

**[01:11:17.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000922)  
sa mga regions natin, kung pwede, unahin po muna namin,

**[01:11:24.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000923)  
bago mag-widen, ayusin po na natin yung mga sira-sira.

**[01:11:27.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000924)  
Kasi unfortunately po, lalo na po sa areas ng Bicol,

**[01:11:32.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000925)  
some areas ng Central Luzon, some areas ng Region 4A,

**[01:11:36.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000926)  
lalo na po sa Quezon, some areas ng Northern Samar at Samar,

**[01:11:41.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000927)  
marami pong talagang sira-sira.

**[01:11:45.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000928)  
At yun po talaga ang pinaprioritize natin.

**[01:11:49.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000929)  
In Mindanao, it's the same.

**[01:11:51.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000930)  
Pero po, pag ayos na naman po yun, then okay na pong mag-widen.

**[01:11:58.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000931)  
Pero ang kailangan nga talaga sa widening, masigurado po na merong right of way.

**[01:12:04.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000932)  
Kasi minsan po, ang napansin din po namin,

**[01:12:08.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000933)  
nag-a-allocate po ng pondo for widening ng Mahalika, pero wala namang right of way.

**[01:12:14.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000934)  
So ang nangyayari po, nawawiden, isang kilometro, biglang napuputol.

**[01:12:18.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000935)  
So while important po talaga yung widening, Mr. Chairman, Minority Leader,

**[01:12:25.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000936)  
we are trying to rationalize it in that way po.

**[01:12:28.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000937)  
But rest assured po, yung Northern Samar to Samar, because these are the most,

**[01:12:32.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000938)  
one of the most dilapidated sections, priority po talaga natin yan, Mr. Chairman, Mr. Minority Leader.

**[01:12:41.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000939)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:12:42.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000940)  
Pangalawa po, Mr. Chairman, ito pong isang napaka-importanting project natin

**[01:12:47.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000941)  
na lagyan natin ng pondo ng 2026 yung for the feasibility study of the Sorsogon-Samar bridge link.

**[01:13:02.960] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000942)  
Pwede bang ma-update kami dito kung ano na yung napaka-vital na connector bridge na ito?

**[01:13:09.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000943)  
Sa Luzon, Samar, Visayas, at Mindanao, napakahalaga po ito sa ating ekonomiya.

**[01:13:15.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000944)  
At kung maaari po, mabrief ninyo kami ng status report kung ano na ang sitwasyon dito sa gagawin nating tulay.

**[01:13:29.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000945)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chairman, Minority Leader.

**[01:13:32.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000946)  
Napaka-importante po ng Matnog-Allen Bridge, or ngayon is the Sorsogon-Northern Samar Bridge.

**[01:13:40.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000947)  
This is a critical link part of the Maharlika network connecting Luzon to the Visayas.

**[01:13:48.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000948)  
This is very close to my heart, and I know very close to the heart of the Minority Leader, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:13:53.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000949)  
Kasi nung ako po ay flagship sekretary po ng previous administration, priority po ito.

**[01:14:00.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000950)  
Unfortunately, during that time, it was thought to be unimplementable

**[01:14:06.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000951)  
because of the depth of the channel in between.

**[01:14:09.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000952)  
Sorsogon and Northern Samar.

**[01:14:12.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000953)  
Pero I am happy po to report today, as promised po to the Minority Leader last time,

**[01:14:19.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000954)  
na we will allocate the much needed feasibility study for this,

**[01:14:24.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000955)  
and we have 120 million pesos allocated for this in the 2027 NEP.

**[01:14:29.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000956)  
And if I may be allowed, Mr. Chairman, to just present to the committee and to the Honorable Minority Leader,

**[01:14:36.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000957)  
meron na pong solusyon.

**[01:14:40.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000958)  
Mr. Chairman, dati po yung original plan, and I'm sure the Minority Leader is very familiar,

**[01:14:46.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000959)  
Diretso po tayo matnog to Allen via the open sea, via the channel.

**[01:14:53.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000960)  
This was...

**[01:14:55.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000961)  
deemed to be unimplementable because of the depth of that channel.

**[01:15:00.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000962)  
Ngayon po, yung designer po, ito po yung designer mismo ng Bataan Cavite Link Bridge,

**[01:15:09.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000963)  
T.Y. Lin of the United States.

**[01:15:10.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000964)  
They're the ones that have built all these iconic bridges all over the country,

**[01:15:15.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000965)  
including the Bay Bridge in San Francisco, many bridges in the Middle East, in Europe.

**[01:15:22.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000966)  
Sila po ang nag-design ng Bataan-Cavite Link Bridge.

**[01:15:25.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000967)  
Sila po ang nakahanap ng solusyon.

**[01:15:27.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000968)  
This is one of the biggest design companies for bridges in the world.

**[01:15:33.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000969)  
They designed it in a way where it will connect to, sorry,

**[01:15:42.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000970)  
Ay sorry po, 130 million po pala ngayong GAA itong feasibility studies.

**[01:15:47.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000971)  
Ngayong 2026, hindi po 2027.

**[01:15:49.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000972)  
130 million for 2026 po pala ito.

**[01:15:54.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000973)  
Nagsisimula na po ito.

**[01:15:55.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000974)  
And kasama na po itong study na ito.

**[01:15:58.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000975)  
Dadaan po tayo sa Capul Island.

**[01:16:02.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000976)  
Tama po ba, Mr. Chairman?

**[01:16:04.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000977)  
I think this is also a major tourist destination.

**[01:16:08.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000978)  
At susunod po sa, anong island yan? Sorry.

**[01:16:15.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000979)  
Dinalupihan Island.

**[01:16:16.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000980)  
And then papasok na po sa Northern Samar.

**[01:16:21.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000981)  
So this not only addresses the engineering challenge,

**[01:16:24.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000982)  
na dadaan sa malalim na tubig,

**[01:16:28.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000983)  
it actually also makes it cheaper.

**[01:16:31.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000984)  
Mas magiging mura po itong tulay na ito

**[01:16:34.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000985)  
dahil po dadaan na tayo dito sa dalawang isla na ito.

**[01:16:37.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000986)  
Similar po dun sa ginawa nilang design sa Bataan Cavite

**[01:16:40.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000987)  
na dumadaan sa Corregidor.

**[01:16:43.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000988)  
May dadaan ng isang isla sa gitna yung Corridor Island.

**[01:16:46.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000989)  
So I'm happy to report po na nagkakalinaw na po tayo

**[01:16:50.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000990)  
dito sa ating Sorsogon Samar Link Bridge.

**[01:16:55.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000991)  
Magandang balita yan, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:16:57.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000992)  
Mukhang malapit na tayo mapunta ng Surigao dito

**[01:17:00.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000993)  
at saka kaya sa Cagayan de Oro.

**[01:17:02.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000994)  
Kaya masayang-masaya ang pinsan ko si Kong Rufus.

**[01:17:07.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000995)  
Mr. Chairman, ilang-ilang...

**[01:17:09.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000996)  
Sorry po, Minority.

**[01:17:11.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000997)  
Speaking of that po, Honorable Chairman,

**[01:17:15.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000998)  
ine-next na po natin yung Southern Leyte to Surigao.

**[01:17:20.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000999)  
Mr. Chairman, may budget ngayon sa NIP natin na

**[01:17:25.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001000)  
P350 million for feasibility study for Mindanao-Leyte Bridge Link.

**[01:17:34.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001001)  
So napakaganda nitong dalawang proyekto.

**[01:17:38.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001002)  
Anyway, ilang kilometro po ba ang nangyayari

**[01:17:41.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001003)  
pag ginawa natin itong Dinalupihan, yung Allen

**[01:17:45.740] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001004)  
Dinalupihan, Capul, Matnog, or Sorsogon Bridge?

**[01:17:51.140] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001005)  
Nagiging ilang kilometro?

**[01:17:52.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001006)  
Together with the...

**[01:17:53.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001007)  
Yung nasa isla...

**[01:17:55.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001008)  
Yung nasa isla po na dadaan sa dalawang isla.

**[01:17:58.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001009)  
It is roughly about 20 kilometers.

**[01:18:00.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001010)  
It will be the...

**[01:18:02.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001011)  
Once it is done,

**[01:18:04.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001012)  
and once the Bataan-Cavite Link Bridge is done,

**[01:18:09.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001013)  
it will be the second longest bridge in the country.

**[01:18:11.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001014)  
Second to Bataan-Cavite Link Bridge.

**[01:18:14.520] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001015)  
So, Mr. Chairman, mas maiksi pala ito

**[01:18:17.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001016)  
kaysa Bataan-Cavite Bridge.

**[01:18:20.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001017)  
Mas maiksi ito.

**[01:18:21.500] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001018)  
20 kilometers lang ito.

**[01:18:23.420] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001019)  
Ang pangatlong tanong, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:18:26.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001020)  
sa economic rate of return,

**[01:18:28.900] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001021)  
itong napaka-importante pag mag-de-decide tayo

**[01:18:31.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001022)  
ng ganitong mega-structures,

**[01:18:34.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001023)  
ano ang mas malaking economic rate of return?

**[01:18:37.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001024)  
Yung Cavite-Bataan Bridge or yung Sorsogon-Samar Bridge Link?

**[01:18:43.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001025)  
Mr. Chairman, maganda po yung...

**[01:18:46.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001026)  
Napakaganda po yung tanong.

**[01:18:47.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001027)  
Again po, Maharlika network po ito

**[01:18:50.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001028)  
kasi Sorsogon ay Bicol to Northern Samar.

**[01:51:15.120] DPWH Project Director Jerry A. Fano** (asr-002321)

**[01:51:22.220] DPWH Project Director Jerry A. Fano** (asr-002322)

**[01:51:32.060] DPWH Project Director Jerry A. Fano** (asr-002324)

**[01:51:41.980] DPWH Project Director Jerry A. Fano** (asr-002326)

**[01:52:03.860] DPWH Project Director Jerry A. Fano** (asr-002327)

Mr. Chairman, yung pong 2025, tama po yung mga datos.

**[02:08:29.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002665)  
Pero meron na ito po tayong sinagawang catch-up plan.

**[02:08:33.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002666)  
At dumadami na po tayo ngayong 2026. At kampante po ang DPWH na yung lahat po ng 5,000 classrooms na nakabudget out of the 13 billion basic education fund for 2025 ay magagawa po by the end of 2026 or bago mag-expire yung pondo ng 2025.

**[02:08:57.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002667)  
Aminado po tayo na talaga pong nadelay. Ang rason po talaga dito and I think this needs to be addressed is yung pong number one, yung pong pagpipirma ng memorandum of agreement between DepEd at DPWH.

**[02:09:20.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002668)  
Na typically po noong 2020, noong mga previous years, napipirmahan na po yan.

**[02:09:27.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002669)  
Mid-year na po. Minsan po umaabot. Kalimbawa yung 2025, napirmahan na po I was informed. August na of 2025, napirmahan ang MOA between DepEd and DPWH.

**[02:09:42.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002670)  
Kung August po napirmahan ang MOA at alam po ni Chairman Romito, ang construction po ng classroom at the earliest, makakasimula po yan, October-November.

**[02:09:56.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002671)  
Kasi po pagkatapos po ng MOA, i-validate pa po ang listahan ng mga classrooms sa iba't ibang lugar at mapapabid pa po yan after two months at makakapagsimula ang construction.

**[02:10:09.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002672)  
At yan po ang nangyayari, hindi lang noong 2025, kundi pati noong previous years. Ngayong pong 2026, minarapat po namin ng pabilisin ito.

**[02:10:24.150] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002673)  
At ngayong 2026 po ay nakapagpirmahan po kami ng MOA nitong April 2026. A few months earlier than August. Medyo delayed pa rin po pero mas maaga po previous year.

**[02:10:39.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002674)  
And dahil po dyan, nagsimula na po tayo ng procurement process natin para sa classrooms ngayong pong Agosto.

**[02:10:53.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002675)

**[02:11:06.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002676)

**[02:12:34.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002691)

**[02:12:37.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002692)

**[02:12:39.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002693)

**[02:12:49.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002694)

**[02:12:51.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002695)

**[02:12:53.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002696)

**[02:12:55.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002697)

**[02:13:03.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002698)

**[02:16:07.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002726)

**[02:16:41.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002729)

**[02:17:10.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002732)

**[02:17:17.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002733)

**[02:17:22.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002735)

**[02:17:24.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002736)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Thank you, Mr. Secretary. Wala po bang ballpark figure man lang?

**[02:17:28.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002737)  
Yes. More than 10, right?

**[02:17:30.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002738)

**[02:17:44.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002739)

**[02:17:48.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002740)

**[02:17:56.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002741)

**[02:18:02.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002742)

**[02:18:16.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002743)

**[02:18:24.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002744)

**[02:18:36.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002745)  
Absolutely po, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:18:38.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002746)

**[02:18:46.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002747)

**[02:18:55.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002748)

**[02:19:04.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002749)

**[02:19:10.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002750)

**[02:19:14.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002751)  
Mr. Chair, gusto ko lang din pong balikan yung issue ng safety and accessibility.

**[02:19:22.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002752)  
Sa ngayon po kasi, isa itong usapin na talagang matindi yung naging gap in the last few years, particularly po sa ating public infrastructure.

**[02:19:32.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002753)  
Kanina lamang po, may ka-meeting ako ng mga PWD leaders sa aking opisina, and lo and behold, when they asked kung may PWD parking ang Kongreso,

**[02:19:39.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002754)  
ang sabi, walang PWD parking, House of Representatives. Nakalulungkot po.

**[02:19:44.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002756)  
Sa isang PWD event po, may na-meet po ako ng isang rapper.

**[02:19:47.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002757)  
Ang pangalang po ng rapper, The Righteous One.

**[02:19:50.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002758)  
At ang sabi po ni The Righteous One, para sa kanila ng mga PWD,

**[02:19:54.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002759)  
ang good governance simply means kapag umaandar ang elevator sa MRT.

**[02:19:59.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002760)  
Alam ko po, hindi kasama ang MRT sa coverage ng area of work ng DPWH.

**[02:20:04.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002761)  
Pero, inuulit ko po, good governance for our PWD siblings is as simple as accessibility infrastructure, for our PWD brothers and sisters.

**[02:20:14.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002763)  
Ano po ang assessment ang DPWH sa compliance sa BP344 or the Accessibility Law, especially across its road and infrastructure projects?

**[02:20:24.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002765)  
Halimbawa po, may datos po ba tayo kung ilang DPWH projects ang compliant sa accessibility standards ng gobyerno?

**[02:20:34.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002766)  
With the permission of the... I will answer this very directly.

**[02:20:37.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002767)  
It's... It's...

**[02:20:42.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002768)  
What word can I use?

**[02:20:44.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002769)  
Terrible compliance to the accessibility law.

**[02:20:48.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002770)  
I mean, kitang-kita naman po natin yung mga kalsada natin, walang sidewalk eh.

**[02:20:52.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002771)  
No, kaya nga po, slowly but surely, pinapakita po natin na pwede natin gawin ng tama.

**[02:20:57.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002772)  
Halimbawa po, EDSA.

**[02:21:02.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002773)  
Ako po mismo, sinamahan ko po yung mga mobility advocates natin, yung mga PWDs natin.

**[02:21:07.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002774)  
Hindi po pwedeng maglakad at mag-commute ang mga PWDs natin sa EDSA.

**[02:21:11.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002775)  
Kasi mamamatay po siya, masasagasaan po siya dyan.

**[02:21:14.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002776)  
But now, with the rehab, we are making sure that we are widening them.

**[02:21:18.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002777)  
Not only widening the sidewalk, but we are providing for the standards that are in the accessibility law.

**[02:21:28.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002778)  
But unfortunately, hindi po ito nai-implement sa buong Pilipinas.

**[02:21:33.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002779)  
And malayo pa po ang kailangan natin marating dito.

**[02:21:36.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002780)  
Pero yun po, kaya nga po, from about 100 million in...

**[02:21:40.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002781)  
170 million

**[02:21:42.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002782)  
last year, for PWD infrastructure, tinaas po natin ito to 500 million this year.

**[02:21:49.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002783)  
Pero kulang pa rin po yan, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:21:51.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002784)  
But ngayon po, ang instructions po natin sa ating mga DEOs at RDs,

**[02:21:55.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002785)  
kailangan po yung mga kagaya natin, kailangan may rampa, kailangan may sidewalk na wide enough.

**[02:22:02.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002786)  
Pero malayo pa po ang ating kailangan marating bago tayo umabot sa full compliance with the accessibility law.

**[02:22:11.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002787)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary. For the very transparent and candid response. Tatanungin ko po sana kung ilang footbridge natin sa Metro Manila ang may functioning elevator. Pero batay po sa sagot nyo, alam ko na agad ang magiging tugon. Nakakarimarim po at nakakabiyak ng puso ito pong failure ng ating public infrastructure para po bigyan ng access ang mga kapatid nating PWD. Inuulit ko po, sabi ni Righteous One, para sa kanya, simple lang ang good governance. May umaandar na elevator sa footbridge, may umaandar na elevator sa MRT. At meron pa po pong nakausap kanina, sabi niya, simple lang ang good governance sa kanila, yung kakasya ang wheelchair sa sidewalk. Apparently, when you go through Metro Manila, maraming sidewalk, hindi kakasya ang wheelchair. So yan po yung nakakarimarim na kalagayan ng public infrastructure and issue of accessibility in this country.

**[02:23:04.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002797)  
Mr. Chairman, we could not agree more with the Honorable Cendaña.

**[02:23:10.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002798)  
Para po sa huling set of questions, Mr. Chair, gusto ko na pong itali from accessibility to issue of health.

**[02:23:16.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002800)  
Ngayon po, parang regular feature na ng mga balita, may isang segment ng road accidents. At pataas po ng pataas ang bilang ng road accidents sa Pilipinas.

**[02:23:25.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002802)  
For example, 13,125 Filipinos died from road crashes in 2023.

**[02:23:32.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002803)  
At sinasabi po ng DOH that the top cause of death among Filipinos aged 15 to 29 years old ay itong mga road crashes and road accidents.

**[02:23:43.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002804)  
Ano po ang ginagawa pagdating sa infrastructure ng DPWH para tiyakin ang safety ng ating mga kababayan? Lalo na po karamihan dito ay mga commuters.

**[02:23:53.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002806)  
Again, Mr. Chairman, napaka-importante po ng tanong.

**[02:23:56.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002807)  
Alam niyo po, if I may be given some time po to answer this because it's very important.

**[02:24:00.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002808)  
Alam niyo po, pag umiikot po ako sa buong bansa, ang isa po sa pinakabinabantayan po ay yung safety standards ng mga ongoing construction.

**[02:24:15.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002810)  
Alam niyo po, nung ako po'y bumisita sa Cordillera, meron po diyang nasira na kalye, iyong  Beckel Road, papuntang Ambuclao mula sa Baguio.

**[02:24:27.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002811)  
Meron pong nahulog dyan na 13-year-old cyclist, namatay po.

**[02:24:32.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002812)  
Sa galit ko po, immediately po, tinanggal ko po yung DE doon dahil hindi po nag-comply ang construction doon sa safety standards na prescribed.

**[02:24:43.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002814)  
Dahil po diyan, and I will ask Undersecretary Lara to give you some details, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:24:50.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002815)  
Pinatagagang pinayos po natin ang safety standards natin at pinapa-implement po natin very strictly with the threat of removal.

**[02:24:58.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002816)  
Kapag po ang mga DEs natin at RDs ay hindi nag-comply at ang ginagawa lang ay caution tape sa mga ongoing projects, tanggal po sila.

**[02:25:12.399] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002817)  
Talaga pong ano eh. Nakakarimarim yung word po na ginamit ni Congressman kanina. Kasi po buhay po ang nakasalalay dito sa safety.

**[02:25:20.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002820)  
If I can just pass on lang po to Usec Lara, Mr. Chairman, para lang po makapagbigay ng konting brief doon sa pagtataas ng standards ng safety sa construction sites.

**[02:25:31.040] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002821)  
Usec Lara, you're recognized.

**[02:25:32.980] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-002822)  
Good morning po, Mr. Chair. Good morning po, the Honorable Congressman.

**[02:25:37.320] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-002823)  
Sa safety standards po, meron na po tayong existing manual but we also want to update this. Yung problem lang po ay hindi na-highlight enough sa mga people on the ground. That's why this semester, we've been conducting trainings po sa ating mga project engineers just to explain to them in detail na iba-ibang klaseng road slips, road collapse, or even just small fixing of potholes. Lahat po ay kailangan ng road safety standards na dependent po sa speed ng road natin, ganun po kalayo ang warning sign natin.

**[02:26:12.360] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-002827)  
Hindi kasi po puwede na ngayon lang lalagyan mo na ng harang. It would cause accidents po.

**[02:26:17.620] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-002829)  
So we are training our project engineers na hindi lang po tayo sa project implementation but also on providing appropriate road safety measures para po sa ating riding public po.

**[02:26:27.940] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-002830)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:26:28.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002831)  
Mr. Chairman, we will provide the updated handbook to the committee para po makita.

**[02:26:34.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002832)  
But I think ang importante nga po dito na i-emphasize is kahit na gano'n po kaganda yung handbook natin, kung ito po ay hindi gagawin.

**[02:26:42.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002833)  
ng ating mga regions at districts, [unclear] wala po iyong handbook na iyan.

**[02:26:47.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002834)  
Kaya ang importante nga po, maintindihan ng lahat na may consequences po kapag meron pong na-aksidente at meron pong namatay.

**[02:26:55.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002835)  
Recently po, sa Naga po, may na-aksidente.

**[02:26:57.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002836)  
Na-ICU po iyong dalawang mag-asawa.

**[02:27:00.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002837)  
Awa ng Diyos, wala pong namatay.

**[02:27:02.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002838)  
Pero yung pong district engineer yun, natanggal din po yun.

**[02:27:05.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002839)  
So there needs to be consequences po, hindi nga po yung pagbabago ng handbook o pagbabago ng standards.

**[02:27:11.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002840)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:27:12.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002841)  
Thank you, Honorable Perci.

**[02:27:14.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002842)

**[02:27:20.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-002844)
